    99:     /// Advance all hover animations. `dt` = seconds since last frame. Returns `true` if still animating.
   100:     pub fn tick_animations(&mut self, dt: f32) -> bool {
   101:         let hl = anim::timing::HOVER;
   102:         // Update targets from current hover state
   103:         self.tab_hover_anim.ensure_len(self.tabs.len());
   104:         self.close_hover_anim.ensure_len(self.tabs.len());
   105:         for i in 0..self.tabs.len() {
   106:             self.tab_hover_anim.set(i, if self.hovered_tab == Some(i) { 1.0 } else { 0.0 });
   107:             self.close_hover_anim.set(i, if self.hovered_close_tab == Some(i) { 1.0 } else { 0.0 });
   108:         }
   109:         self.active_anim.ensure_len(self.tabs.len());
   110:         for i in 0..self.tabs.len() {
   111:             self.active_anim.set(i, if self.tabs[i].active { 1.0 } else { 0.0 });
   112:         }
   113:         self.new_tab_anim.set(if self.hovered_new_tab { 1.0 } else { 0.0 });
   114:         self.btn_minimize_anim.set(if self.hovered_button == Some(WindowButton::Minimize) { 1.0 } else { 0.0 });
   115:         self.btn_maximize_anim.set(if self.hovered_button == Some(WindowButton::Maximize) { 1.0 } else { 0.0 });
   116:         self.btn_close_anim.set(if self.hovered_button == Some(WindowButton::Close) { 1.0 } else { 0.0 });
   117: 
   118:         let mut animating = false;
   119:         animating |= self.tab_hover_anim.tick(hl, dt);
   120:         animating |= self.close_hover_anim.tick(hl, dt);
   121:         animating |= self.active_anim.tick(anim::timing::HOVER, dt);
   122:         animating |= self.new_tab_anim.tick(hl, dt);
   123:         animating |= self.btn_minimize_anim.tick(hl, dt);
   124:         animating |= self.btn_maximize_anim.tick(hl, dt);
   125:         animating |= self.btn_close_anim.tick(hl, dt);
   126: 
   127:         // Breathing glow on active tab accent (~3.5s cycle, frame-rate independent)
   128:         if self.tabs.iter().any(|t| t.active) {
   129:             self.glow_phase += dt * std::f32::consts::TAU / 3.5;
   130:             if self.glow_phase > std::f32::consts::TAU { self.glow_phase -= std::f32::consts::TAU; }
   131:             animating = true;
   132:         }
   133: 
   134:         animating
   135:     }
   136: 
   137:     fn layout(&self, bar: Rect, scale: f32) -> TabBarLayout {
   138:         self.layout_engine
   139:             .borrow_mut()
   140:             .compute(
   141:                 bar,
   142:                 self.sidebar_width,
   143:                 self.tabs.len(),
   144:                 scale,
   145:                 TabBarLayoutConfig {
   146:                     show_brand: self.show_brand,
   147:                     show_indicators: self.show_indicators,
   148:                     show_controls: self.show_window_controls,
   149:                     show_new_tab: self.show_new_tab_button,
   150:                 },
   151:             )
   152:     }
   153: 
   154:     /// Current breathing glow phase (for sharing with other components).
   155:     pub fn glow_phase(&self) -> f32 { self.glow_phase }
   156: 
   157:     pub fn accent_for(&self, index: usize) -> [f32; 4] {
   158:         self.tabs.get(index)
   159:             .and_then(|t| t.accent)
   160:             .unwrap_or_else(|| TAB_ACCENTS[index % TAB_ACCENTS.len()])
   161:     }
   162: 
   163:     pub fn build(&self, ui: &mut UiBuilder, bar: Rect, text: &UiTextRenderer) {
   164:         let s = |v: f32| text.s(v);
   165:         let cw = text.cell_width;
   166:         let ch = text.cell_height;
   167:         let text_y = |area_h: f32, y: f32| y + (area_h - ch) / 2.0;
   168:         let layout = self.layout(bar, text.scale);
   169: 
   170:         // Background ÔÇö flat fill matching web reference (background: #0f1117)
   171:         ui.fill(bar, colors::BG_RAISED);
   172: 
   173:         // (No top accent stripe ÔÇö clean flat bar matching web reference)
   174: 
   175:         // Bottom separator ÔÇö solid 1px hairline matching web reference
   176:         // (borderBottom: "1px solid #1a1d25").
   177:         ui.hline_aa(bar.x, bar.bottom() - 1.0, bar.width, 1.0, colors::BORDER);
   178: 
   179:         // Sidebar section: "Godly Terminal" branding
   180:         if self.show_brand && layout.brand.width > 0.0 {
   181:             // Flat background matching sidebar tone (web reference: same #0b0d12)
   182:             let brand_section = layout.brand;
   183:             ui.fill(brand_section, colors::BG_DARK);
   184:             let icon_size = ch * 1.1;
   185:             let icon_x = brand_section.x + s(10.0);
   186:             let icon_y = brand_section.y + (brand_section.height - icon_size) / 2.0;
   187:             let brand_x = icon_x + icon_size + s(6.0);
   188:             let brand_y = text_y(brand_section.height, brand_section.y);
   189:             // Branding text with subtle accent tint from active tab color
   190:             let active_accent = self.tabs.iter().enumerate()
   191:                 .find(|(_, t)| t.active)
   192:                 .map(|(i, _)| self.accent_for(i))
   193:                 .unwrap_or(colors::ACCENT_BLUE);
   194:             let brand_fg = [
   195:                 colors::FG_SECONDARY[0] * 0.85 + active_accent[0] * 0.15,
   196:                 colors::FG_SECONDARY[1] * 0.85 + active_accent[1] * 0.15,
   197:                 colors::FG_SECONDARY[2] * 0.85 + active_accent[2] * 0.15,
   198:                 colors::FG_SECONDARY[3],
   199:             ];
   200:             // Terminal icon with accent tint
   201:             let icon_fg = [
   202:                 colors::FG_MUTED[0] * 0.7 + active_accent[0] * 0.3,
   203:                 colors::FG_MUTED[1] * 0.7 + active_accent[1] * 0.3,
   204:                 colors::FG_MUTED[2] * 0.7 + active_accent[2] * 0.3,
   205:                 0.6,
   206:             ];
   207:             let icon_t = (1.2 * text.scale).max(1.0);
   208:             ui.icon_terminal(
   209:                 Rect { x: icon_x, y: icon_y, width: icon_size, height: icon_size },
   210:                 icon_t, icon_fg,
   211:             );
   212:             ui.text_ui_bold(text, "Godly Terminal", brand_x, brand_y,
   213:                     brand_fg, colors::BG_DARK);
   214:             // Right border for sidebar section ÔÇö solid hairline matching web
   215:             ui.vline(brand_section.right() - 1.0, brand_section.y, brand_section.height, 1.0, colors::BORDER);
   216:         }
   217: 
   218:         // Icon line thickness (used for close buttons in tabs and window controls)
   219:         let icon_t = (ICON_LINE_T * text.scale).max(1.0);
   220: 
   221:         // Close button dimensions (reserved on right side of every tab)
   222:         let close_btn_sz = if self.show_tab_close_buttons { s(16.0) } else { 0.0 };
   223:         let close_btn_pad = if self.show_tab_close_buttons { s(8.0) } else { 0.0 };
   224: 
   225:         // Proportional UI font advance for tab title width estimation.
   226:         // Using ui_avg_advance gives tighter truncation than monospace cell_width
   227:         // because proportional characters are narrower on average (~75% of cell_width).
   228:         let ui_cw = if text.ui_avg_advance > 0.0 { text.ui_avg_advance } else { cw * 0.75 };
   229: 
   230:         // Title metrics within each retained-layout tab slot.
   231:         let badge_sz = s(18.0);
   232:         let title_x_offset = s(10.0) + badge_sz + s(6.0);
   233: 
   234:         for (i, tab) in self.tabs.iter().enumerate() {
   235:             let accent = self.accent_for(i);
   236:             let hover_t = self.tab_hover_anim.get(i); // 0.0 ÔåÆ 1.0 smooth
   237:             let active_t = self.active_anim.get(i);
   238: 
   239:             let rect = layout.tabs[i];
   240:             let title_max_w = rect.width - title_x_offset - close_btn_sz - close_btn_pad - s(4.0);
   241:             let title_max_chars = (title_max_w / ui_cw).floor().max(1.0) as usize;
   242: 
   243:             // Tab background ÔÇö web reference style: flat rects, no rounded-top gradients.
   244:             // Active: #161920 background + 2px colored bottom border
   245:             // Hover: subtle background lift
   246:             // Inactive: transparent
   247:             let bg = lerp_color(
   248:                 lerp_color(colors::BG_RAISED, colors::BG_SURFACE, hover_t),
   249:                 colors::BG_TAB_ACTIVE,
   250:                 active_t,
   251:             );
   252: 
   253:             if active_t > 0.005 {
   254:                 // Active: flat background matching web (#161920)
   255:                 let active_bg = [colors::BG_TAB_ACTIVE[0], colors::BG_TAB_ACTIVE[1], colors::BG_TAB_ACTIVE[2], active_t];
   256:                 ui.fill(rect, active_bg);
   257: 
   258:                 // Bottom accent indicator (2px colored bar) ÔÇö web: borderBottom: 2px solid ${color}
   259:                 let accent_color = [accent[0], accent[1], accent[2], active_t];
   260:                 let bottom_bar = Rect {

   533:                             [colors::BORDER[0], colors::BORDER[1], colors::BORDER[2], 0.4 * btn_t],
   534:                         )
   535:                     };
   536:                     let hover_top = [base_color[0] * 1.12, base_color[1] * 1.12, base_color[2] * 1.12, base_color[3]];
   537:                     ui.fill_rounded_gradient(*rect, hover_top, base_color, s(3.0));
   538:                     ui.stroke_rounded(*rect, s(3.0), 0.5, border_color);
   539:                 }
   540: 
   541:                 let icon_color = if *btn == WindowButton::Close {
   542:                     lerp_color(colors::FG_DIM, colors::WHITE, btn_t)
   543:                 } else {
   544:                     lerp_color(colors::FG_DIM, colors::FG_SECONDARY, btn_t)
   545:                 };
   546:                 match btn {
   547:                     WindowButton::Minimize => ui.icon_minimize(*rect, s(10.0), icon_t, icon_color),
   548:                     WindowButton::Maximize => ui.icon_maximize(*rect, s(9.0), icon_t, icon_color),
   549:                     WindowButton::Close => ui.icon_x(*rect, s(9.0), icon_t, icon_color),
   550:                 }
   551:             }
   552:         }
   553:     }
   554: 
   555:     pub fn on_mouse(&mut self, event: MouseEvent, bar: Rect, scale: f32) -> Option<UiAction> {
   556:         let layout = self.layout(bar, scale);
   557:         match event {
   558:             MouseEvent::Move { x, y } => {
   559:                 self.hovered_tab = None;
   560:                 self.hovered_close_tab = None;
   561:                 self.hovered_button = None;
   562:                 self.hovered_new_tab = self.show_new_tab_button && layout.new_tab.contains(x, y);
   563:                 let close_btn_sz = 16.0 * scale;
   564:                 let close_btn_pad = 8.0 * scale;
   565:                 for (i, tab) in self.tabs.iter().enumerate() {
   566:                     let rect = layout.tabs[i];
   567:                     if rect.contains(x, y) {
   568:                         self.hovered_tab = Some(i);
   569:                         // Check close button hover (visible on active + hovered tabs)
   570:                         let show_close = tab.active || true; // hovered_tab is already Some(i)
   571:                         if show_close {
   572:                         if self.show_tab_close_buttons {
   573:                             let close_rect = Rect {
   574:                                 x: rect.right() - close_btn_sz - close_btn_pad,
   575:                                 y: rect.y + (rect.height - close_btn_sz) / 2.0,
   576:                                 width: close_btn_sz,
   577:                                 height: close_btn_sz,
   578:                             };
   579:                             if close_rect.contains(x, y) {
   580:                                 self.hovered_close_tab = Some(i);
   581:                             }
   582:                         }
   583:                     }
   584:                 }
   585:                 }
   586:                 if self.show_window_controls {
   587:                     for (rect, btn) in &[
   588:                         (layout.buttons[0], WindowButton::Minimize),
   589:                         (layout.buttons[1], WindowButton::Maximize),
   590:                         (layout.buttons[2], WindowButton::Close),
   591:                     ] {
   592:                         if rect.contains(x, y) {
   593:                             self.hovered_button = Some(*btn);
   594:                         }
   595:                     }
   596:                 }
   597:                 None
   598:             }
   599:             MouseEvent::Press { x, y } => {
   600:                 // Check window buttons first
   601:                 if self.show_window_controls {
   602:                     for (rect, btn) in &[
   603:                         (layout.buttons[0], WindowButton::Minimize),
   604:                         (layout.buttons[1], WindowButton::Maximize),
   605:                         (layout.buttons[2], WindowButton::Close),
   606:                     ] {
   607:                         if rect.contains(x, y) {
   608:                             return match btn {
   609:                                 WindowButton::Close => Some(UiAction::CloseWindow),
   610:                                 WindowButton::Minimize => Some(UiAction::MinimizeWindow),
   611:                                 WindowButton::Maximize => Some(UiAction::MaximizeWindow),
   612:                             };
   613:                         }
   614:                     }
   615:                 }
   616:                 // Check tabs ÔÇö close button takes priority over tab switch
   617:                 let close_btn_sz = 16.0 * scale;
   618:                 let close_btn_pad = 8.0 * scale;
   619:                 for (i, tab) in self.tabs.iter().enumerate() {
   620:                     let rect = layout.tabs[i];
   621:                     if rect.contains(x, y) {
   622:                         // Check if click landed on the close button area
   623:                         if self.show_tab_close_buttons {
   624:                             let close_rect = Rect {
   625:                                 x: rect.right() - close_btn_sz - close_btn_pad,
   626:                                 y: rect.y + (rect.height - close_btn_sz) / 2.0,
   627:                                 width: close_btn_sz,
   628:                                 height: close_btn_sz,
   629:                             };
   630:                             if close_rect.contains(x, y) {
   631:                                 return Some(UiAction::CloseTab(tab.id.clone()));
   632:                             }
   633:                         }
   634:                         return Some(UiAction::SwitchTab(tab.id.clone()));
   635:                     }
   636:                 }
   637:                 if self.show_new_tab_button && layout.new_tab.contains(x, y) {
   638:                     return Some(UiAction::NewTab);
   639:                 }
   640:                 // Click on empty tab bar area = drag window
   641:                 if bar.contains(x, y) {
   642:                     return Some(UiAction::DragWindow);
   643:                 }
   644:                 None
   645:             }
   646:             _ => None,
   647:         }
   648:     }
   649: }

   398:     fn ensure_tab_slots(&mut self, tab_count: usize) {
   399:         while self.tab_slots.len() < tab_count {
   400:             let node = self.tree.new_leaf(Style::default()).expect("tab slot node");

   405:         children.push(self.nodes.new_tab_slot);
   406:         self.tree
   407:             .set_children(self.nodes.tabs_strip, &children)
   408:             .expect("tabs strip children");
   409:     }

   414:             .expect("tab bar layout style should update");
   415:     }

    47: /// Retained flexbox tree for shell chrome.
    48: ///
    49: /// The old shell layout path was a pile of manual rectangle math. Keeping the
    50: /// top-level regions in a persistent taffy tree gives us a real layout layer we

   377:         self.tree
   378:             .set_style(node, style)
   379:             .expect("layout node style should update");
   380:     }
   381: 
   382:     fn absolute_rect_for(&self, path: &[NodeId]) -> Rect {
   383:         let mut x = 0.0;
   384:         let mut y = 0.0;
   385:         let mut width = 0.0;
   386:         let mut height = 0.0;
   387: 
   388:         for node in path {
   389:             let layout = self.tree.layout(*node).expect("layout node should exist");
   390:             x += layout.location.x;
   391:             y += layout.location.y;
   392:             width = layout.size.width;
   393:             height = layout.size.height;
   394:         }
   395: 
   396:         Rect {
   397:             x,
   398:             y,
   399:             width,
   400:             height,
   401:         }
   402:     }
   403: }
   404:

   408:     }

  ### src-tauri/native/terminal-surface/src/font_metrics.rs:1-220
     1: /// Font metrics for monospace terminal rendering.
     2: ///
     3: /// Cell dimensions are measured from actual font tables (hhea/OS/2 ascent,
     4: /// descent, line gap, and the advance width of a reference glyph). Heuristic
     5: /// ratios are only used as a last-resort fallback when font data cannot be
     6: /// parsed.
     7: ///
     8: /// All pixel dimensions (`cell_width`, `cell_height`, `baseline_offset`,
     9: /// `font_size`) are in **logical** pixels. Call [`scaled_for_render`] to
    10: /// obtain physical-pixel metrics for the pixel renderer path.
    11: #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq)]
    12: pub struct FontMetrics {
    13:     /// Width of a single cell in pixels.
    14:     pub cell_width: f32,
    15:     /// Height of a single cell in pixels.
    16:     pub cell_height: f32,
    17:     /// Font size in pixels.
    18:     pub font_size: f32,
    19:     /// Vertical offset from cell top to text baseline in pixels.
    20:     pub baseline_offset: f32,
    21:     /// DPI scale factor from the OS (e.g. 1.0, 1.25, 1.5, 2.0).
    22:     /// Defaults to 1.0. Used by the pixel renderer to rasterize glyphs
    23:     /// at the correct physical resolution.
    24:     pub scale_factor: f32,
    25: }
    26: 
    27: impl FontMetrics {
    28:     /// Width-to-font-size ratio for monospace fonts (last-resort heuristic).
    29:     const WIDTH_RATIO: f32 = 0.6;
    30:     /// Height-to-font-size ratio for monospace fonts (last-resort heuristic).
    31:     const HEIGHT_RATIO: f32 = 1.3;
    32:     /// Baseline position as fraction of cell height (last-resort heuristic).
    33:     const BASELINE_FRACTION: f32 = 0.75;
    34: 
    35:     /// Create font metrics from a font size using heuristic ratios.
    36:     ///
    37:     /// **This is a last-resort fallback.** Prefer [`from_font_bytes`] or
    38:     /// [`from_system_font`] which read actual font tables.
    39:     ///
    40:     /// - `cell_width = font_size * 0.6`
    41:     /// - `cell_height = font_size * 1.3`
    42:     /// - `baseline_offset = cell_height * 0.75`
    43:     pub fn from_font_size(font_size: f32) -> Self {
    44:         log::warn!(
    45:             "FontMetrics: using heuristic fallback for font_size={:.1}px \
    46:              ÔÇö metrics will be approximate",
    47:             font_size,
    48:         );
    49:         Self::from_font_size_quiet(font_size)
    50:     }
    51: 
    52:     /// Heuristic fallback without the warning (used internally after an
    53:     /// explicit warning has already been emitted, and in `Default`).
    54:     fn from_font_size_quiet(font_size: f32) -> Self {
    55:         let cell_width = font_size * Self::WIDTH_RATIO;
    56:         let cell_height = font_size * Self::HEIGHT_RATIO;
    57:         let baseline_offset = cell_height * Self::BASELINE_FRACTION;
    58:         Self {
    59:             cell_width,
    60:             cell_height,
    61:             font_size,
    62:             baseline_offset,
    63:             scale_factor: 1.0,
    64:         }
    65:     }
    66: 
    67:     /// Create font metrics by measuring the actual font file via swash.
    68:     ///
    69:     /// Reads ascent, descent, and leading from the font's OS/2 and hhea tables,
    70:     /// and measures the advance width of a reference glyph ('0', 'M', or space)
    71:     /// for the cell width. Falls back to heuristic ratios (with a warning) if
    72:     /// the font data cannot be parsed.
    73:     pub fn from_font_bytes(font_size: f32, font_data: &[u8]) -> Self {
    74:         let font = match swash::FontRef::from_index(font_data, 0) {
    75:             Some(f) => f,
    76:             None => {
    77:                 log::warn!(
    78:                     "FontMetrics: failed to parse font data ({} bytes) \
    79:                      ÔÇö falling back to heuristics for font_size={:.1}px",
    80:                     font_data.len(),
    81:                     font_size,
    82:                 );
    83:                 return Self::from_font_size_quiet(font_size);
    84:             }
    85:         };
    86: 
    87:         let metrics = font.metrics(&[]);
    88:         let upem = metrics.units_per_em as f32;
    89:         if upem == 0.0 {
    90:             log::warn!(
    91:                 "FontMetrics: font reports units_per_em=0 \
    92:                  ÔÇö falling back to heuristics for font_size={:.1}px",
    93:                 font_size,
    94:             );
    95:             return Self::from_font_size_quiet(font_size);
    96:         }
    97: 
    98:         let scale = font_size / upem;
    99:         let ascent = metrics.ascent * scale;
   100:         let descent = metrics.descent.abs() * scale;
   101:         let leading = metrics.leading * scale;
   102: 
   103:         let cell_height = ascent + descent + leading;
   104:         let baseline_offset = ascent;
   105: 
   106:         // Measure advance width of a reference glyph for cell width.
   107:         // Try '0' first (standard monospace reference), then 'M', then space.
   108:         let charmap = font.charmap();
   109:         let glyph_metrics = font.glyph_metrics(&[]).scale(font_size);
   110: 
   111:         let cell_width = {
   112:             let candidates = ['0', 'M', ' '];
   113:             let mut width = 0.0f32;
   114:             for &ch in &candidates {
   115:                 let gid = charmap.map(ch);
   116:                 if gid != 0 {
   117:                     let w = glyph_metrics.advance_width(gid);
   118:                     if w > 0.0 {
   119:                         width = w;
   120:                         break;
   121:                     }
   122:                 }
   123:             }
   124:             width
   125:         };
   126: 
   127:         // Sanity check: if measurements look unreasonable, fall back
   128:         if cell_width <= 0.0 || cell_height <= 0.0 || baseline_offset <= 0.0 {
   129:             log::warn!(
   130:                 "FontMetrics: measured values out of range \
   131:                  (cell_width={:.2}, cell_height={:.2}, baseline={:.2}) \
   132:                  ÔÇö falling back to heuristics for font_size={:.1}px",
   133:                 cell_width,
   134:                 cell_height,
   135:                 baseline_offset,
   136:                 font_size,
   137:             );
   138:             return Self::from_font_size_quiet(font_size);
   139:         }
   140: 
   141:         Self {
   142:             cell_width,
   143:             cell_height,
   144:             font_size,
   145:             baseline_offset,
   146:             scale_factor: 1.0,
   147:         }
   148:     }
   149: 
   150:     /// Create font metrics by loading a system font by family name.
   151:     ///
   152:     /// Uses [`FontLoader`](crate::font_loader::FontLoader) to locate the font
   153:     /// on disk, then delegates to [`from_font_bytes`](Self::from_font_bytes)
   154:     /// for actual measurement. Falls back to heuristic ratios (with a warning)
   155:     /// if the font cannot be found.
   156:     pub fn from_system_font(font_size: f32, family: &str) -> Self {
   157:         let loader = crate::font_loader::FontLoader::new();
   158:         match loader.load_font(family, false, false) {
   159:             Some(data) => Self::from_font_bytes(font_size, &data),
   160:             None => {
   161:                 log::warn!(
   162:                     "FontMetrics: could not load system font '{}' \
   163:                      ÔÇö falling back to heuristics for font_size={:.1}px",
   164:                     family,
   165:                     font_size,
   166:                 );
   167:                 Self::from_font_size_quiet(font_size)
   168:             }
   169:         }
   170:     }
   171: 
   172:     /// Return a copy with all pixel dimensions multiplied by `scale_factor`.
   173:     ///
   174:     /// The pixel renderer uses this to rasterize glyphs and lay out cells at
   175:     /// the physical-pixel resolution required by the OS DPI setting.
   176:     /// When `scale_factor == 1.0` the returned metrics are identical.
   177:     pub fn scaled_for_render(&self) -> Self {
   178:         let s = self.scale_factor;
   179:         Self {
   180:             cell_width: self.cell_width * s,
   181:             cell_height: self.cell_height * s,
   182:             font_size: self.font_size * s,
   183:             baseline_offset: self.baseline_offset * s,
   184:             scale_factor: s,
   185:         }
   186:     }
   187: 
   188:     /// Set the DPI scale factor. Returns `self` for chaining.
   189:     pub fn with_scale_factor(mut self, scale_factor: f32) -> Self {
   190:         self.scale_factor = scale_factor;
   191:         self
   192:     }
   193: }
   194: 
   195: impl Default for FontMetrics {
   196:     fn default() -> Self {
   197:         Self::from_font_size_quiet(14.0)
   198:     }
   199: }

   332:         let m = FontMetrics::from_font_size(14.0).with_scale_factor(1.5);
   333:         let s = m.scaled_for_render();
   334:         assert!((s.cell_width - 14.0 * 0.6 * 1.5).abs() < 0.01);
   335:         assert!((s.font_size - 14.0 * 1.5).abs() < 0.01);
   336:     }
   337:

   339:     fn scaled_for_render_at_1x_is_identity() {
   340:         let m = FontMetrics::from_font_size(14.0);
   341:         let s = m.scaled_for_render();
   342:         assert_eq!(m.cell_width, s.cell_width);
   343:         assert_eq!(m.cell_height, s.cell_height);
   344:         assert_eq!(m.font_size, s.font_size);
   345:         assert_eq!(m.baseline_offset, s.baseline_offset);
   346:     }

   352:         let fallback = heuristic(14.0);
   353:         assert_ne!(
   354:             measured, fallback,
   355:             "measured metrics from a real font should differ from heuristic"

   361:         // baseline_offset should be the font's ascent, not a heuristic fraction
   362:         let m = FontMetrics::from_font_bytes(14.0, GEIST_MONO);
   363:         // For Geist Mono, ascent-based baseline should be roughly 70-80% of
   364:         // cell height, not exactly 75% (the heuristic). Verify reasonable range.
   365:         let ratio = m.baseline_offset / m.cell_height;
   366:         assert!(
   367:             (0.5..0.95).contains(&ratio),
   368:             "baseline/height ratio ({:.3}) should be in 0.5..0.95 for a real font",
   369:             ratio,
   370:         );

   384:     fn terminal_size(&self) -> (u16, u16) {
   385:         if let (Some(gpu), Some(renderer)) = (&self.gpu, &self.renderer) {
   386:             let metrics = renderer.font_metrics().scaled_for_render();
   387:             let vw = gpu.config.width as f32;
   388:             let vh = gpu.config.height as f32;
   389:             let layout = self.shell_layout(vw, vh);
   390:             let cols = (layout.terminal_content.width / metrics.cell_width).floor() as u16;
   391:             let rows = (layout.terminal_content.height / metrics.cell_height).floor() as u16;
   392:             (rows.max(1), cols.max(1))
   393:         } else {
   394:             (24, 80)
   395:         }
   396:     }
   397: 
   398:     fn shell_layout(&self, viewport_w: f32, viewport_h: f32) -> ui::layout::ShellLayout {
   399:         self.layout_engine.borrow_mut().compute(
   400:             viewport_w,
   401:             viewport_h,
   402:             true,
   403:             self.right_panel.visible && !self.is_web_reference_crop(),
   404:             !self.is_web_reference_crop(),
   405:             self.sidebar_width,
   406:             self.right_panel_width,
   407:             self.scale_factor,
   408:         )
   409:     }

  1976:     fn window_event(
  1977:         &mut self,
  1978:         event_loop: &ActiveEventLoop,
  1979:         _id: WindowId,
  1980:         event: WindowEvent,
  1981:     ) {
  1982:         match event {
  1983:             WindowEvent::CloseRequested => event_loop.exit(),
  1984:             WindowEvent::Resized(size) => {
  1985:                 // Track maximized state to skip outer shadow rendering
  1986:                 if let Some(w) = &self.window {
  1987:                     self.is_maximized = w.is_maximized();
  1988:                     self.scale_factor = w.scale_factor() as f32;
  1989:                 }
  1990:                 if let Some(gpu) = &mut self.gpu {
  1991:                     let surface_size = self
  1992:                         .window
  1993:                         .as_ref()
  1994:                         .map_or(size, |window| window_surface_size(window));
  1995:                     if surface_size != size {
  1996:                         log::info!(
  1997:                             "[DPI] resize surface: winit={}x{}, hwnd-client={}x{}, scale={}",
  1998:                             size.width,
  1999:                             size.height,
  2000:                             surface_size.width,
  2001:                             surface_size.height,
  2002:                             self.scale_factor,
  2003:                         );
  2004:                     }
  2005:                     gpu.config.width = surface_size.width.max(1);
  2006:                     gpu.config.height = surface_size.height.max(1);
  2007:                     gpu.surface.configure(&gpu.device, &gpu.config);
  2008:                 }
  2009:                 // Resize the terminal session
  2010:                 if self.daemon.is_some() && self.active_session.is_some() {
  2011:                     let (rows, cols) = self.terminal_size();
  2012:                     let daemon = Arc::clone(self.daemon.as_ref().unwrap());
  2013:                     let session_id = self.active_session.clone().unwrap();
  2014:                     std::thread::spawn(move || {
  2015:                         let _ = daemon.send_request(&Request::Resize {
  2016:                             session_id,
  2017:                             rows,
  2018:                             cols,
  2019:                         });
  2020:                     });
  2021:                 }
  2022:                 if let Some(w) = &self.window {
  2023:                     w.request_redraw();
  2024:                 }
  2025:             }
  2026:             WindowEvent::Focused(focused) => {
  2027:                 self.window_focused = focused;
  2028:                 self.status_bar.window_focused = focused;
  2029:                 self.focus_dim_anim.set(if focused { 0.0 } else { 1.0 });
  2030:                 if let Some(w) = &self.window { w.request_redraw(); }
  2031:             }
  2032:             WindowEvent::RedrawRequested => {
  2033:                 self.render();
  2034:             }
  2035:             WindowEvent::ModifiersChanged(mods) => {
  2036:                 self.modifiers = mods;
  2037:             }

  2041:                 let gpu = self.gpu.as_ref();
  2042:                 let (vw, vh) = gpu.map(|g| (g.config.width as f32, g.config.height as f32)).unwrap_or((1200.0, 800.0));
  2043:                 let layout = self.shell_layout(vw, vh);

  2049:                     self.sidebar_width = (px / self.scale_factor).clamp(min_w / self.scale_factor, max_w / self.scale_factor);
  2050:                     if let Some(w) = &self.window { w.request_redraw(); }

  2055:                     let max_w = 550.0 * self.scale_factor;

  2058:                     if let Some(w) = &self.window { w.request_redraw(); }
  2059:                     return;
  2060:                 }

  2065:                 let handle_y_max = layout.status_bar.y;
  2066:                 let in_handle_y = py >= handle_y_min && py <= handle_y_max;

   211: function InlineContent({ children }) {
   212:   if (!children) return null;
   213:   return (
   214:     <span>
   215:       {children.map((c, i) => {
   216:         if (c.link) {
   217:           return (
   218:             <span key={i} style={{ color: "#58a6ff", textDecoration: "underline", cursor: "pointer" }}>
   219:               {c.text}
   220:             </span>
   221:           );
   222:         }
   223:         if (c.code) {
   224:           return (
   225:             <span
   226:               key={i}
   227:               style={{
   228:                 backgroundColor: "#1c2030",
   229:                 color: "#e6a855",
   230:                 padding: "1px 5px",
   231:                 borderRadius: 3,
   232:                 fontSize: 12,
   233:                 fontFamily: "inherit",
   234:               }}
   235:             >
   236:               {c.text}
   237:             </span>
   238:           );
   239:         }
   240:         return <span key={i}>{c.text}</span>;
   241:       })}
   242:     </span>
   243:   );
   244: }

   246: function RichBlock({ block }) {
   247:   if (block.type === "heading") {
   248:     return (
   249:       <h3
   250:         style={{
   251:           color: "#e6edf3",
   252:           fontSize: 14,
   253:           fontWeight: 700,
   254:           margin: "18px 0 8px",
   255:           fontFamily: "inherit",
   256:           letterSpacing: 0.2,
   257:         }}
   258:       >
   259:         {block.text}
   260:       </h3>
   261:     );
   262:   }
   263: 
   264:   if (block.type === "bullet") {
   265:     return (
   266:       <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, margin: "4px 0", lineHeight: 1.55, fontSize: 13 }}>
   267:         <span style={{ color: "#6e7681", flexShrink: 0 }}>ÔÇó</span>
   268:         <div>
   269:           {block.children ? <InlineContent children={block.children} /> : <span>{block.text}</span>}
   270:           {block.sub && (
   271:             <div style={{ paddingLeft: 12, marginTop: 2 }}>
   272:               {block.sub.map((s, i) => (
   273:                 <div key={i} style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, color: "#8b949e", fontSize: 12, padding: "1px 0" }}>
   274:                   <span style={{ color: "#484f58" }}>ÔÇó</span>
   275:                   <span>{s}</span>
   276:                 </div>
   277:               ))}
   278:             </div>
   279:           )}
   280:         </div>
   281:       </div>
   282:     );
   283:   }
   284: 
   285:   if (block.type === "numbered") {
   286:     return (
   287:       <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, margin: "4px 0", lineHeight: 1.55, fontSize: 13 }}>
   288:         <span style={{ color: "#6e7681", flexShrink: 0 }}>1.</span>
   289:         <span>
   290:           <InlineContent children={block.children} />
   291:         </span>
   292:       </div>
   293:     );
   294:   }
   295: 
   296:   if (block.type === "paragraph") {
   297:     return (
   298:       <p style={{ margin: "8px 0", lineHeight: 1.6, fontSize: 13, color: "#c9d1d9" }}>{block.text}</p>
   299:     );
   300:   }
   301: 
   302:   if (block.type === "user-message") {
   303:     return (
   304:       <div
   305:         style={{
   306:           borderLeft: "3px solid #6366f1",
   307:           backgroundColor: "#14171f",
   308:           padding: "6px 12px",
   309:           margin: "6px 0",
   310:           borderRadius: "0 4px 4px 0",
   311:           color: block.highlight || "#c9d1d9",
   312:           fontSize: 13,
   313:           fontWeight: 500,
   314:         }}
   315:       >
   316:         {block.text}
   317:       </div>
   318:     );
   319:   }
   320: 
   321:   if (block.type === "thoughts") {
   322:     return <ThoughtsBlock count={block.count} items={block.items} />;
   323:   }
   324: 
   325:   if (block.type === "command") {
   326:     return (
   327:       <div
   328:         style={{
   329:           backgroundColor: "#0a0c10",
   330:           border: "1px solid #1e2128",
   331:           borderRadius: 5,
   332:           padding: "6px 10px",
   333:           margin: "8px 0",
   334:           fontSize: 12,
   335:           color: "#8b949e",
   336:           display: "flex",
   337:           alignItems: "center",
   338:           gap: 6,
   339:         }}
   340:       >
   341:         <span style={{ color: "#6e7681", fontFamily: "inherit" }}>{block.text}</span>
   342:         <span style={{ color: "#3b4048", marginLeft: "auto" }}>Ôû©</span>
   343:       </div>
   344:     );
   345:   }
   346: 
   347:   if (block.type === "editing") {
   348:     return (
   349:       <div
   350:         style={{
   351:           display: "flex",
   352:           alignItems: "center",
   353:           gap: 6,

   362:       </div>
   363:     );
   364:   }
   365: 
   366:   return null;
   367: }

   438:     fn pixel_to_grid(&self, x: f64, y: f64) -> godly_terminal_surface::GridPos {
   439:         if let Some(renderer) = &self.renderer {
   440:             let phys = renderer.font_metrics().scaled_for_render();
   441:             let col = (x as f32 / phys.cell_width).floor() as usize;
   442:             let row = (y as f32 / phys.cell_height).floor() as usize;
   443:             godly_terminal_surface::GridPos { row, col }
   444:         } else {
   445:             godly_terminal_surface::GridPos { row: 0, col: 0 }
   446:         }
   447:     }

   449:     fn scroll(&mut self, delta: isize) {
   450:         if self.is_web_reference_crop() {
   451:             return;
   452:         }
   453:         let new_offset = (self.scrollback_offset as isize + delta).max(0) as usize;
   454:         if new_offset == self.scrollback_offset { return; }

  1705:                             if px >= layout.terminal_content.x + layout.terminal_content.width
  1706:                                 || py >= layout.terminal_content.y + layout.terminal_content.height
  1707:                             {
  1708:                                 continue;
  1709:                             }
  1710: 
  1711:                             // Corner radii: round outer edges of the selection shape
  1712:                             let is_single = sel_start.row == sel_end.row;
  1713:                             let is_first = row == sel_start.row;
  1714:                             let is_last = row == sel_end.row;
  1715:                             let radii = if is_single {
  1716:                                 [radius; 4]
  1717:                             } else if is_first {
  1718:                                 [radius, radius, 0.0, 0.0]
  1719:                             } else if is_last {
  1720:                                 [0.0, 0.0, radius, radius]
  1721:                             } else {
  1722:                                 [0.0; 4]
  1723:                             };
  1724: 
  1725:                             sel_verts.extend_from_slice(
  1726:                                 &ui::quad_renderer::quad_vertices_sdf(
  1727:                                     px, py, pw, ph,
  1728:                                     vw, vh, sel_color,
  1729:                                     radii, 0.5, sel_border, 0.0,
  1730:                                 ),
  1731:                             );
  1732:                         }
  1733: 
  1734:                         if !sel_verts.is_empty() {
  1735:                             quad_pipe.draw(&gpu.device, &gpu.queue, &mut pass, &sel_verts);
  1736:                         }
  1737:                     }
  1738:                 }
  1739:             }
  1740: 
  1741:             // Draw cursor overlay via SDF quad pipeline (on top of terminal text)
  1742:             // for rounded corners and subtle glow ÔÇö professional look.
  1743:             if let Some(quad_pipe) = &mut self.quad_pipeline {
  1744:                 if let Some(grid) = &self.current_grid {
  1745:                     if !grid.cursor_hidden {
  1746:                         if let Some(r) = &self.renderer {
  1747:                             let m = r.font_metrics().scaled_for_render();
  1748:                             let cw = m.cell_width;
  1749:                             let ch = m.cell_height;
  1750:                             let cpx = (grid.cursor.col as f32 * cw).round() + layout.terminal_content.x;
  1751:                             let cpy = (grid.cursor.row as f32 * ch).round() + layout.terminal_content.y;
  1752: 
  1753:                             // Clip cursor to terminal area
  1754:                             let clip_r = layout.terminal_content.x + layout.terminal_content.width;
  1755:                             let clip_b = layout.terminal_content.y + layout.terminal_content.height;
  1756:                             if cpx < clip_r && cpy < clip_b {
  1757:                                 use godly_protocol::types::CursorShape;
  1758:                                 let blink_t = self.cursor_blink_anim.value();
  1759:                                 let accent = active_accent;
  1760:                                 let radius = 2.0 * self.scale_factor;
  1761:                                 let focused = self.window_focused;
  1762: 
  1763:                                 let (cx, cy, cwidth, cheight) = match grid.cursor.cursor_style {
  1764:                                     CursorShape::BlinkBlock | CursorShape::SteadyBlock => {
  1765:                                         (cpx, cpy, cw, ch)
  1766:                                     }
  1767:                                     CursorShape::BlinkUnderline | CursorShape::SteadyUnderline => {
  1768:                                         let uh = (2.0 * self.scale_factor).max(2.0);
  1769:                                         (cpx, cpy + ch - uh, cw, uh)
  1770:                                     }
  1771:                                     CursorShape::BlinkBar | CursorShape::SteadyBar => {
  1772:                                         let bw = (2.0 * self.scale_factor).max(2.0);
  1773:                                         (cpx, cpy, bw, ch)
  1774:                                     }
  1775:                                 };
  1776: 
  1777:                                 let is_block = matches!(grid.cursor.cursor_style,
  1778:                                     CursorShape::BlinkBlock | CursorShape::SteadyBlock);
